I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

[STATEMENT]

My goal is to have that object c craves object a.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Here is the sequence of human-readable actions to achieve the goal:

1.  **Object d feasts upon Object c.**
    *   *Explanation:* This action is performed because 'd' craves 'c', 'd' is a province, and harmony currently exists.
    *   *Outcome:* Object 'd' begins to experience pain, 'c' becomes a province, 'd' no longer craves 'c', 'd' is no longer a province, and the state of harmony is lost.

2.  **Object d succumbs.**
    *   *Explanation:* This action is performed because 'd' is currently in pain.
    *   *Outcome:* Object 'd' is no longer in pain, 'd' regains its status as a province, 'd' becomes a planet, and harmony is restored.

3.  **Object c attacks.**
    *   *Explanation:* This action is performed because 'c' is a province, 'c' is a planet, and harmony currently exists.
    *   *Outcome:* Object 'c' begins to experience pain, 'c' loses its status as a province and a planet, and the state of harmony is lost.

4.  **Object c overcomes Object a.**
    *   *Explanation:* This action is performed because 'a' is a province and 'c' is currently in pain.
    *   *Outcome:* Harmony is restored, 'c' becomes a province, 'c' now craves 'a', 'a' is no longer a province, and 'c' is no longer in pain.

The goal state of **Object c craving Object a** has been achieved.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Here is the sequence of actions translated into human-readable instructions, following the provided plan:

1.  **Feast D upon A:** Entity D, currently craving A, residing in a province, and in a state of harmony, will now feast upon A. As a result, D will experience pain, A will become a province, D will no longer crave A, D will lose its provincial status, and harmony will be broken.
2.  **Succumb D:** Entity D, currently experiencing pain, will succumb. Consequently, D will gain provincial and planetary status, harmony will be restored, and D's pain will cease.
3.  **Feast A upon C:** Entity A, currently craving C, residing in a province, and in a state of harmony, will feast upon C. This action will cause A to experience pain, C to become a province, A to stop craving C, A to lose its provincial status, and harmony to be disrupted.
4.  **Overcome A over D:** Entity A, experiencing pain, will overcome Entity D, which is currently a province. This act will restore harmony, grant provincial status to A, make A crave D, remove provincial status from D, and alleviate A's pain.
5.  **Feast C upon B:** Entity C, currently craving B, residing in a province, and in a state of harmony, will feast upon B. As a consequence, C will feel pain, B will become a province, C will no longer crave B, C will lose its provincial status, and harmony will be broken.
6.  **Succumb C:** Entity C, currently in pain, will succumb. This will make C a province and a planet, restore harmony, and remove C's pain.
7.  **Feast A upon D:** Entity A, currently craving D, residing in a province, and in a state of harmony, will feast upon D. This action will result in A experiencing pain, D becoming a province, A no longer craving D, A losing its provincial status, and harmony being disrupted.
8.  **Succumb A:** Entity A, currently in pain, will succumb. This act will grant A provincial and planetary status, restore harmony, and relieve A of its pain.
9.  **Attack D:** Entity D, currently a province and a planet, and in a state of harmony, will be attacked. As a result, D will feel pain, lose its provincial and planetary status, and harmony will be lost.
10. **Overcome D over B:** Entity D, experiencing pain, will overcome Entity B, which is currently a province. This action will restore harmony, grant provincial status to D, make D crave B, remove provincial status from B, and alleviate D's pain.
11. **Attack A:** Entity A, currently a province and a planet, and in a state of harmony, will be attacked. Consequently, A will experience pain, lose its provincial and planetary status, and harmony will be broken.
12. **Overcome A over D:** Entity A, experiencing pain, will overcome Entity D, which is currently a province. This act will restore harmony, grant provincial status to A, make A crave D, remove provincial status from D, and alleviate A's pain.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Here is the sequence of human-readable actions:

1.  **Feast d a**: Object d feasts on object a, which causes d pain, makes a a province, and disrupts harmony.
2.  **Succumb d**: Object d succumbs, ending its pain, restoring it to a province/planet, and bringing back harmony.
3.  **Feast a c**: Object a feasts on object c, which causes a pain, makes c a province, and disrupts harmony.
4.  **Succumb a**: Object a succumbs, ending its pain, restoring it to a province/planet, and bringing back harmony.
5.  **Feast c b**: Object c feasts on object b, which causes c pain, makes b a province, and disrupts harmony.
6.  **Succumb c**: Object c succumbs, ending its pain, restoring it to a province/planet, and bringing back harmony.
7.  **Attack a**: Object a attacks, causing it pain and disrupting harmony.
8.  **Overcome a b**: Object a overcomes its pain by focusing on object b, making a crave b, restoring a to a province, and bringing back harmony.
9.  **Attack d**: Object d attacks, causing it pain and disrupting harmony.
10. **Overcome d c**: Object d overcomes its pain by focusing on object c, making d crave c, restoring d to a province, and bringing back harmony.
================================================================================

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Here is the sequence of human-readable actions derived from the plan:

1.  **Feast (b, d):** Object b performs a Feast on object d. This action causes object b to experience pain, object d to become a province, while object b ceases to be a province, and the craving of b for d, along with the general harmony, is removed.
2.  **Overcome (b, c):** Object b attempts to Overcome object c. This action restores harmony, makes object b a province, and creates a craving of b for c, while object c ceases to be a province and object b's pain is relieved.
3.  **Attack (d):** Object d is subjected to an Attack. This action causes object d to experience pain, while it ceases to be a province and a planet, and the general harmony is removed.
4.  **Overcome (d, a):** Object d attempts to Overcome object a. This action restores harmony, makes object d a province, and creates a craving of d for a, while object a ceases to be a province and object d's pain is relieved.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Here is the sequence of actions to achieve the goal:

1.  **Object c feasts on object b.**
    *   As a result, object c begins to experience pain.
    *   Object b becomes a province.
    *   Object c no longer craves object b.
    *   Object c loses its province status.
    *   Harmony is no longer active.

2.  **Object c succumbs.**
    *   As a result, object c's province status is restored.
    *   Object c becomes a planet.
    *   Harmony is restored.
    *   Object c's pain is removed.

3.  **Object d feasts on object a.**
    *   As a result, object d begins to experience pain.
    *   Object a becomes a province.
    *   Object d no longer craves object a.
    *   Object d loses its province status.
    *   Harmony is no longer active.

4.  **Object d succumbs.**
    *   As a result, object d's province status is restored.
    *   Object d becomes a planet.
    *   Harmony is restored.
    *   Object d's pain is removed.

5.  **Object a is attacked.**
    *   As a result, object a begins to experience pain.
    *   Object a loses its province status.
    *   Object a loses its planet status.
    *   Harmony is no longer active.

6.  **Object a overcomes object b.**
    *   As a result, harmony is restored.
    *   Object a becomes a province.
    *   Object a now craves object b. (The goal is achieved!)
    *   Object b loses its province status.
    *   Object a's pain is removed.
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, object a craves object b, object b craves object c, object c craves object d, harmony, planet object d and province object a.
My goal is to have that object b craves object c, object c craves object d and object d craves object a.

My plan is as follows:

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
